## Phase 1: Foundation — Bundle Contract, Manifest, Atomic Layout

### Step 1: Define the bundle-based env contract (`runpod-lifecycle/src/runpod_lifecycle/prebuilt.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** the module with:
   - `@dataclass(frozen=True) PrebuiltEnvContract`: `volume_name`, `data_center_id`, `mount_path` (default `/workspace`), `cache_root` (`{mount_path}/reigh-livetest-prebuilt`), `runtime_venv_path` (default `/opt/reigh-worker-live-test-venv`, **container disk**), `runtime_worker_path` (default `/workspace-local/reigh-worker`, container disk via `--container-disk` mount), `runtime_vibecomfy_path` (default `/workspace-local/vibecomfy`, container disk), `models_path` (`{cache_root}/models`, **stays on volume**), `attention_profile` (`portable`|`sage`), `comfyui_pin`, `python_version` (e.g. `3.11`), `bundle_format_version` (int).
   - `@dataclass(frozen=True) PrebuiltManifest`: `schema_version: int` (bump = mandatory rebuild), `bundle_format_version: int`, `built_at_utc: str`, `built_by: str` (operator id / hostname for audit), `pyproject_hash: str`, `custom_nodes_lock_hash: str`, `comfyui_pin: str`, `attention_profile: str`, `python_version: str`, `cuda_extra: str` (literal `cuda124`), `vibecomfy_commit: str`, `reigh_worker_commit: str`, `uv_version: str`, `venv_bundle_sha256: str`, `vibecomfy_bundle_sha256: str`, `models_index_sha256: str` (sha of `models/INDEX.json` listing path + size + sha for each model), `notes: str`.
   - `compute_pyproject_hash(text)`, `compute_lockfile_hash(text)` (SHA256 of canonicalized newline-normalized content).
   - `manifest_path(contract) -> str` returns `{cache_root}/env.manifest.json`.
   - `lock_path(contract) -> str` returns `{cache_root}/build.lock` (used to serialize concurrent builds — addresses scope-1).
   - `staging_path(contract) -> str` returns `{cache_root}/.staging-{uuid}` for atomic rename (addresses scope-1).
2. **Document** invalidation rules in module docstring with this precedence:
   - **HARD-FAIL → operator rebuild required**: `schema_version` drift, `bundle_format_version` drift, `python_version` drift, `cuda_extra` drift.
   - **Delta-sync acceptable** (resync against existing extracted bundle): `pyproject_hash`, `custom_nodes_lock_hash`, `comfyui_pin` drift; or `vibecomfy_commit` / `reigh_worker_commit` drift (just git checkout).
   - **No-op**: all hashes match.
   This addresses correctness-4 (python_version) and correctness-5 (schema_version).
3. **Export** all symbols from `runpod_lifecycle/__init__.py`.

### Step 2: SSH-side helpers (`runpod-lifecycle/src/runpod_lifecycle/prebuilt.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `read_manifest(ssh, contract) -> PrebuiltManifest | None` (cat + JSON parse; `None` on missing/corrupt).
2. **Add** `write_manifest(ssh, contract, manifest)` (write via heredoc to a temp file then `mv` atomically into place).
3. **Add** `acquire_build_lock(ssh, contract, *, holder_id, ttl_sec=7200)` — creates `lock_path` with `O_EXCL`; embeds `holder_id` + acquired-at timestamp; returns a release callback. If existing lock is fresher than TTL → raise with the recorded holder id + age; if older than TTL → log a warning, take over.
4. **Add** `verify_extracted_env(ssh, contract) -> list[str]` that runs three cheap probes and returns a list of detected partial-state issues (empty == healthy): (a) `python -c "import torch; print(torch.version.cuda)"` matches manifest; (b) `test -f {runtime_vibecomfy_path}/template_index.json && test -f {runtime_vibecomfy_path}/workflow_corpus/manifests/coverage.json`; (c) `du -sb {runtime_venv_path}/lib | awk` is within 20% of the manifest-recorded venv size. **This addresses issue_hints-2** (partial-state diagnostics).

### Step 3: Refactor install helpers into bundle-aware primitives (`reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/ssh_bootstrap.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Extract** the inner shell of `run_install` (`ssh_bootstrap.py:166-196`) into private `_uv_sync_shell(workdir, *, env_path, extras=("cuda124",), with_locked=False)` — the **extras tuple is mandatory and non-empty** (addresses correctness-3). `run_install` calls it with `env_path="/opt/reigh-worker-live-test-venv"` and `extras=("cuda124",)`, preserving the exact current command.
2. **Extract** the body of `clone_and_install_vibecomfy` (`ssh_bootstrap.py:199-236`) into `_vibecomfy_install_shell(workdir, *, python_path, attention_profile, run_nodes_restore=True)`. Public function is unchanged — addresses the "still passes existing tests" criterion.
3. **Add** new bundle helpers:
   - `bundle_venv(ssh, *, source_env_path, bundle_path)` — `zstd -1 --threads=0` then sha256 on the producer side; writes to `bundle_path` on the volume via atomic rename through `staging_path`.
   - `bundle_install_tree(ssh, *, source_path, bundle_path)` — same for vibecomfy/comfyui trees.
   - `extract_bundle_to_container_disk(ssh, *, bundle_path, target_path, expected_sha256)` — verifies sha, extracts with `pv` for progress, fails with the first/last 50 lines of stderr on mismatch.
   - `ensure_git_ref_synced(ssh, *, workdir, repo_url, ref, force_clone=False)` — used by consumer to set the worker/vibecomfy ref. On force_clone: full clone. Else: `git fetch && git checkout ref && git reset --hard FETCH_HEAD && git clean -ffd`. **Does not re-run `uv sync` or `pip install -e` unless the caller asks** (consumer decides based on manifest drift).
4. **Re-implement** `run_install` and `clone_and_install_vibecomfy` as thin wrappers calling `_uv_sync_shell` / `_vibecomfy_install_shell` with the original arguments. Observable shell output must remain byte-identical except where Python whitespace is normalized.
5. **Decision** for the destructive `vibecomfy.cli nodes restore` call (addresses callers-2): consumer runs `nodes restore` **only when `custom_nodes_lock_hash` drifts**. Otherwise the extracted `custom_nodes/` tree is reused as-is. The full-install wrapper still always restores. Make this explicit in the helper signature: `_vibecomfy_install_shell(..., run_nodes_restore=True)`; consumer passes `run_nodes_restore=lock_hash_drifted`.

### Step 4: Parameterize the worker launch command (`reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/launch_command.py`, `tests/test_primitives.py`)
**Scope:** Small (high impact — addresses FLAG-002 / correctness-2 / all_locations-1)
1. **Change** `build_run_worker_command` signature to take `venv_path: str = "/opt/reigh-worker-live-test-venv"` (keyword-only, default preserves current behavior).
2. **Use** `venv_path` in the `UV_PROJECT_ENVIRONMENT` export at `launch_command.py:37`.
3. **Update** call site in `variant_fresh.py:457-464` to omit the kwarg (default keeps existing behavior); call site in `variant_prebuilt.py` will pass the contract's `runtime_venv_path`.
4. **Update** assertions in `reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/tests/test_primitives.py` lines 870, 2317-2319, 2445: change the literal-string assertion to assert the `UV_PROJECT_ENVIRONMENT=` prefix + parameterized path. Add one new test that calling `build_run_worker_command(venv_path="/custom")` emits `UV_PROJECT_ENVIRONMENT="/custom"`.

### Step 5: Extract shared helpers from `variant_fresh.py` (`reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/_shared.py`)
**Scope:** Medium (addresses scope-3)
1. **Create** `_shared.py` with: `prepare_live_test_context(args) -> dict` (the body of `variant_fresh._prepare_context`), `validate_cases(cases, project_id)`, `register_worker_record(db, pod_id, pod, args, *, variant_label)`, `select_network_volume(api_key, candidate_names) -> tuple[id, name] | (None, None)`, `resolve_runpod_gpu_type_id`, `_phase` context manager, `_capture_and_redact_noisy_lifecycle_output`, `_redact_sensitive_text`, `_runs_root`, `_timestamp_label`. These move out of `variant_fresh.py` verbatim.
2. **Update** `variant_fresh.py` to import from `_shared`. Behavior is unchanged; this is a pure relocation.
3. **Add** `select_network_volume` parameter `data_center_filter: str | None = None` for region-pinning support used by Step 7.

## Phase 2: Builder — Atomic Volume Provisioner with Lock + Multi-Region

### Step 6: Add `prebuilt` CLI verb (`runpod-lifecycle/src/runpod_lifecycle/cli.py`)
**Scope:** Medium (addresses all_locations-3, scope-2)
1. **Inspect** existing subparser registration in `cli.py` (Read first; the plan assumes argparse subparsers — adapt to whatever framework is in use).
2. **Register** new subparsers: `prebuilt build`, `prebuilt inspect`, `prebuilt invalidate`, `prebuilt list`.
3. **`prebuilt build` args:** `--volume-name` (required), `--data-center` (required; e.g. `EU-NO-1`), `--attention-profile {portable,sage}` (default portable), `--worker-ref` (default `main`), `--vibecomfy-ref` (default `main`), `--gpu-type` (default `NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4090`), `--container-disk-gb` (default 200; **never reduce below 200 — addresses callers-3 — the venv extract target lives on container disk**), `--volume-disk-gb` (default 500), `--dry-run`, `--force` (override lock).
4. **Builder flow** (one phase per `_phase` block, mirroring `variant_fresh.py:189-211`):
   - `provision_builder_pod`: create pod with **name prefix `reigh-livetest-builder-`** (addresses correctness-6 / scope-2); attach named volume; require matching `data_center_id`.
   - `acquire_lock`: call `acquire_build_lock`; on failure abort with the holder id printed.
   - `clone_repos`: clone `reigh-worker` to `/opt/build/reigh-worker`, `vibecomfy` to `/opt/build/vibecomfy` (container disk, not the volume).
   - `install_worker`: `_uv_sync_shell(...)` with `env_path=/opt/reigh-worker-live-test-venv`.
   - `install_vibecomfy`: `_vibecomfy_install_shell(...)` with `run_nodes_restore=True`.
   - `bundle_artifacts`: `bundle_venv` + `bundle_install_tree` → staging dir on volume, sha256 each, then atomic `mv` to final paths.
   - `seed_models_dir`: `mkdir -p {models_path}`, write empty `INDEX.json` if absent; **do not pre-download model weights** (out of scope for v1 — model warming is a separate operator concern, but the volume layout is reserved).
   - `write_manifest`: compute and write the manifest.
   - `release_lock`.
   - `terminate_builder_pod`: same `guarded_terminate` path used by consumers.
5. **Builder pod cleanup integration** (addresses scope-2): extend `terminate_guard.LIVE_TEST_FRESH_POD_PREFIX` to a tuple `LIVE_TEST_POD_PREFIXES = ("reigh-live-test-fresh-", "reigh-livetest-prebuilt-", "reigh-livetest-builder-")` and update `prune_stale_live_test_pods` to match any of them. The runpod-lifecycle CLI imports the prune helper (or duplicates the prefix list — concrete decision: import path is `from scripts.live_test.terminate_guard import prune_stale_live_test_pods`; if runpod-lifecycle cannot import reigh-worker code, replicate the helper as `runpod_lifecycle.guard.prune_pods_by_prefix(prefixes, ...)` and have `terminate_guard.py` delegate to it).
6. **`prebuilt inspect`**: provision a small pod (or reuse existing SSH if `--pod` provided), `read_manifest`, print formatted output, terminate.
7. **`prebuilt invalidate`**: `rm -rf {cache_root}/{venv.tar.zst,vibecomfy.tar.zst,env.manifest.json}` (preserve `models/`).
8. **`prebuilt list`**: enumerate `runpod_lifecycle.get_network_volumes` and `cat` each one's manifest (cheap if a small probe pod is reused, else list-only without manifest content).

### Step 7: Multi-region volume convention (`runpod-lifecycle/src/runpod_lifecycle/prebuilt.py`, `reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/config.py`)
**Scope:** Small (addresses all_locations-2)
1. **Volume naming convention**: `reigh-livetest-prebuilt-{profile}-{region-suffix}` where region-suffix mirrors `RUNPOD_STORAGE_VOLUMES` (`peter`, `eu-no-1`, `eu-cz-1`, `eur-is-1`). Builder operator runs `prebuilt build` once per region they want covered.
2. **Consumer-side resolution** (`config.py`): add `PREBUILT_VOLUME_CANDIDATES = tuple(f"reigh-livetest-prebuilt-portable-{region.lower()}" for region in RUNPOD_STORAGE_VOLUMES)`. Consumer walks this list; the first existing volume + its `dataCenterId` win, and the pod is created GPU-pinned to that region.
3. **Document** in the new doc section: "the prebuilt volume is region-pinned; build at least one region; consumer auto-selects".

## Phase 3: Consumer — `variant_prebuilt.py` with Bundle Extraction, Auto-Detect, Diagnostics

### Step 8: New consumer variant (`reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/variant_prebuilt.py`)
**Scope:** Medium (addresses scope-3 via Step 5 extraction)
1. **Create** the module importing from `_shared`. The skeleton mirrors `variant_fresh.run`: prepare context → validate cases → create pod → SSH → bootstrap → launch worker → matrix → report → guarded terminate.
2. **Pod creation differences**:
   - Pod name prefix: `reigh-livetest-prebuilt-`.
   - Volume selection: `select_network_volume(candidate_names=PREBUILT_VOLUME_CANDIDATES, data_center_filter=None)`. Fail-fast with `prebuilt build` command if zero volumes found.
   - GPU region-pin: use the volume's `dataCenterId` to constrain `find_gpu_type` if possible; if the SDK does not expose region-pinned GPU lookup, log a warning and let pod create fail loudly (still better than mounting the wrong region).
   - Container disk: **keep at 200 GB** (callers-3); reject any `--container-disk-gb` value below 100.
3. **Bootstrap phases** (each wrapped in `_phase`):
   - `attach_prebuilt_volume` — confirm volume mount via `mountpoint -q`.
   - `read_prebuilt_manifest` — `read_manifest`. If `None`: raise with the **exact** `rl prebuilt build --volume-name X --data-center Y --attention-profile portable` command.
   - `verify_prebuilt_manifest` — compute local manifest, call `manifest_drift`. On **hard-fail drift** (`schema_version`/`bundle_format_version`/`python_version`/`cuda_extra` mismatch): raise with the `rl prebuilt build` command — **never delta-sync** (addresses correctness-4 / correctness-5).
   - `extract_venv_bundle` — `extract_bundle_to_container_disk` to `/opt/reigh-worker-live-test-venv`.
   - `extract_vibecomfy_bundle` — extract to container-local `/workspace-local/vibecomfy`.
   - `extract_comfyui_bundle` — same for ComfyUI install tree.
   - `verify_extracted_env` — run the partial-state probes (Step 2.4); on any issue, raise with a numbered diagnostic and the `rl prebuilt invalidate && rl prebuilt build` instruction (addresses issue_hints-2).
   - `sync_worker_ref` — `ensure_git_ref_synced` for `reigh-worker` to `args.ref`. If `pyproject_hash` drifted: run `_uv_sync_shell(extras=("cuda124",))` against the extracted venv. **`--extra cuda124` is mandatory in this path** (addresses correctness-3).
   - `sync_vibecomfy_ref` — `ensure_git_ref_synced` for `vibecomfy` to `args.vibecomfy_ref`. If `custom_nodes_lock_hash` drifted: re-run `nodes restore`. Otherwise reuse the extracted `custom_nodes/`. If `vibecomfy_commit` differs only: just `pip install -e` against the new tree.
   - `bind_models_dir` — export `HF_HOME={models_path}/huggingface`, `HF_HUB_CACHE={models_path}/huggingface/hub`, and the ComfyUI models env (`COMFYUI_MODELS_PATH` or whatever the fork uses — confirm by reading vibecomfy/ComfyUI config first; if no single env var exists, write a `extra_model_paths.yaml` pointing at `{models_path}/comfyui`). **Addresses FLAG-008 / issue_hints-1**: models live on the volume and are reused across runs; consumer never re-downloads.
   - `launch_worker` — call `build_run_worker_command(..., venv_path=contract.runtime_venv_path)` so the worker process uses the same venv (addresses FLAG-002).
4. **Manifest update**: after a successful delta sync that changed `pyproject_hash` or `custom_nodes_lock_hash` or git refs, write back an updated manifest with the new hashes and a `notes` line recording the delta. Acquired under `acquire_build_lock` to avoid stomping a concurrent builder.
5. **Strict mode**: `--strict-prebuilt` flag aborts on any drift (including soft) without syncing — useful for CI reproducibility checks.

### Step 9: CLI dispatch and auto-detect (`reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/main.py`, `config.py`)
**Scope:** Small (addresses issue_hints-3)
1. **Dispatch**: add `prebuilt` to the variant switch in `main.py` alongside `fresh` / `update`. Use the existing `--variant` flag pattern; do NOT change the default flag value (still `fresh`) — but…
2. **Add `--variant auto` (new default)**: pre-flights `select_network_volume(PREBUILT_VOLUME_CANDIDATES)`. If any volume exists AND its manifest is readable → dispatch to `prebuilt`. Else → dispatch to `fresh` and emit a structured `prebuilt_unavailable` log line explaining how to build the volume. This is the **discovery-by-default-AND-used-by-default** mechanism (addresses issue_hints-3) without forcing operators into a non-existent prebuilt path.
3. **Flags added**: `--prebuilt-volume-name`, `--strict-prebuilt`, `--allow-delta` (default true).
4. **Reject** any operator attempt to set `--container-disk-gb < 100` for the prebuilt variant (addresses callers-3 / `project_live_test_disk.md` memory).
5. **Update** the `--variant` default in `main.py` argparse to `auto`; existing operator invocations that pass `--variant fresh` keep working unchanged.

### Step 10: Pod-prefix and termination wiring (`reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/terminate_guard.py`)
**Scope:** Small (addresses correctness-6 / scope-2)
1. **Replace** `LIVE_TEST_FRESH_POD_PREFIX = "reigh-live-test-fresh-"` with `LIVE_TEST_POD_PREFIXES: tuple[str, ...] = ("reigh-live-test-fresh-", "reigh-livetest-prebuilt-", "reigh-livetest-builder-")`.
2. **Update** `_FRESH_POD_NAME_RE` to a list of patterns, one per prefix, and broaden `prune_stale_live_test_pods` to iterate all of them.
3. **Preserve** existing `guarded_terminate` semantics — it acts on a single pod id passed in; only the prune helper needed broadening.

## Phase 4: Tests, Real Validation, Docs/Skills

### Step 11: Local tests (`runpod-lifecycle/tests/test_prebuilt.py`, `reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/tests/test_variant_prebuilt.py`, updates to `test_primitives.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Unit tests for `prebuilt.py`**: hash determinism; manifest round-trip; drift detection per field; **schema_version drift returns the dedicated `hard_fail` reason** (verified test); **python_version drift returns `hard_fail`** (verified test); lockfile acquire/release; lock takeover after TTL.
2. **Unit tests for `ssh_bootstrap.py` refactor**: with a fake SSH client capturing every command — `run_install` emits the **byte-identical** command to today (golden string compare). `_uv_sync_shell(extras=())` raises (`extras` is mandatory non-empty). New `ensure_git_ref_synced` emits the expected `git fetch && git checkout && git reset --hard && git clean -ffd` sequence without any `uv sync` unless the caller wires it.
3. **Harness tests for `variant_prebuilt`**: manifest match → zero install commands; `pyproject_hash` drift → `_uv_sync_shell(extras=("cuda124",))` issued; `custom_nodes_lock_hash` drift → `nodes restore` issued; missing manifest → `RuntimeError` containing the literal substring `rl prebuilt build --volume-name`; `schema_version` drift → `RuntimeError` (no sync attempted) containing `rl prebuilt build`.
4. **Bundle-extract test**: fake SSH with a captured `tar`/`zstd` command sequence; verify sha256 check is in the executed shell.
5. **`launch_command` parameterization test**: `build_run_worker_command(venv_path="/x")` emits `UV_PROJECT_ENVIRONMENT="/x"`; update the four existing assertions at `test_primitives.py:870, 2317-2319, 2445`.
6. **Dry-run test for `--variant prebuilt`** and **`--variant auto`**: assert auto falls back to fresh when no volume exists (with a fake `select_network_volume` returning `None`); auto picks prebuilt when one exists.

### Step 12: Real RunPod validation (operator)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Build** once: `rl prebuilt build --volume-name reigh-livetest-prebuilt-portable-eu-no-1 --data-center EU-NO-1 --attention-profile portable --worker-ref main --vibecomfy-ref main`. Capture wall-clock for builder phases.
2. **Run** consumer twice (single small case, e.g. `z_image_turbo`):
   - Run A: clean — `python -m scripts.live_test --variant prebuilt --backend vibecomfy --case z_image_turbo`. Measure pod-create → `launch_worker` elapsed.
   - Run B: 5 min later, same command. Measures steady-state (model cache reuse).
3. **Assert** Run A bootstrap (pod-create → `launch_worker`) finishes in **under 10 min** vs ~67 min cold. Run B model phase reuses cached weights (verified by absence of HF download log lines for the model).
4. **Verify** post-run via `runpod-cli list` (or equivalent) that only the consumer pod was terminated; the builder pod is already gone; the prebuilt volume persists.
5. **Capture** the run log under `reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/runs/{timestamp}/` and append a markdown summary `prebuilt-validation.md` with elapsed phase times.

### Step 13: Documentation and skill discovery (committed, not auto-memory) (`docs/migration-vibecomfy-live-validation.md`, `reigh-worker/.claude/skills/live-test/SKILL.md`, `vibecomfy/CLAUDE.md`)
**Scope:** Small (addresses all_locations-4, issue_hints-3)
1. **`docs/migration-vibecomfy-live-validation.md`**: add a "Prebuilt validation environment" section covering: when to use, how `--variant auto` picks it, `rl prebuilt build|inspect|invalidate` reference, hard-fail vs delta-sync drift rules, multi-region naming, model-cache layout, partial-state diagnostics, concurrent-builder lock behavior.
2. **`reigh-worker/.claude/skills/live-test/SKILL.md`** (new file, committed): names the prebuilt path as default, links to the doc above. This file is repo-resident so any agent cloning the repo gets the same guidance.
3. **`vibecomfy/CLAUDE.md`**: one-line addition in the decision-shortcuts section pointing at the live-test skill.
4. **No auto-memory entry** is used as the durable surface (addresses all_locations-4); auto-memory can still record a personal pointer but the committed skill is the contract.

## Execution Order

1. **Phase 1 first**: contract + manifest + helper extraction (Steps 1–5). `variant_fresh.py` keeps working unchanged — verified by Step 11.2 golden-string tests before any consumer code is written.
2. **Phase 2 second**: builder CLI is independently usable; we can build the volume before any consumer code exists.
3. **Phase 3 third**: new consumer variant + auto-detect dispatch.
4. **Phase 4 last**: tests in Step 11 are written alongside Phases 1–3 (test-driven where cheap); Step 12 real run after Step 11 passes; Step 13 docs at the end.

## Validation Order

1. **Cheap first**: unit tests for `prebuilt.py` and the `ssh_bootstrap.py` golden-string regression test (Step 11.1–11.2).
2. **Dry-run**: `--variant prebuilt --dry-run` and `--variant auto --dry-run` (Step 11.6) — no RunPod credentials needed.
3. **Test fixture update**: ensure `test_primitives.py` still passes after the launch-command parameterization (Step 11.5).
4. **Real RunPod**: Step 12 — single case, two runs (cold-bootstrap-from-bundles + steady-state).
5. **Full matrix run** (info-only operator step, after validation succeeds).

  "questions": [
    "MooseFS sequential read throughput for multi-GB tar.zst bundles is the only unverified architectural assumption. If Step 12's measured extraction time exceeds ~10 minutes we'd want to revisit hosting bundles on a non-MooseFS persistent backing or pre-staging them on the runpod template. Is the operator willing to fall back to a Docker-image variant if MooseFS streaming is too slow, or should we hard-commit to bundles and absorb whatever cost MooseFS imposes?",
    "Should --variant auto truly become the new default in main.py, or stay as an opt-in flag value with --variant fresh remaining default? Auto-default has the strongest discovery-by-default semantics but risks surprising operators who set up a prebuilt volume for a different attention profile.",
    "Model warming: out of scope for v1 per the plan, but should we add an optional rl prebuilt warm-models <recipe> verb that pre-downloads weights for a named subset of workflows? It would be a small extension and the largest remaining cost driver after deps.",
    "Should prebuilt inspect / list be allowed to ssh into a running consumer pod rather than provision a small probe pod (saving GPU minutes)? The plan provisions a probe pod for simplicity."
  ],

  "assumptions": [
    "The runpod base pytorch image provides 200+ GB of container-local disk; the extracted venv (~15-25 GB) plus checkouts fit comfortably and leave room for runtime outputs.",
    "MooseFS sequential read throughput is acceptable for streaming a multi-GB tar.zst (we benchmark this in Step 12 \u2014 if it isn't, we'd reconsider hosting bundles on a non-MooseFS volume, but this is the v1 hypothesis).",
    "RunPod network volumes are region-pinned, so a multi-region rollout requires one build per region. v1 ships one region (whatever the operator builds first); auto-detect lists the rest in priority order via PREBUILT_VOLUME_CANDIDATES.",
    "variant_update.py stays untouched: it is a deliberate operator path that already optimizes by skipping clone work, and conflating it with prebuilt would muddy the contract.",
    "Concurrent builders are rare (multi-operator but not high-frequency); a single O_EXCL lockfile + TTL takeover is sufficient \u2014 no need for distributed coordination.",
    "Model warming is a separate operator concern in v1 \u2014 the manifest reserves the models/ layout but does not pre-populate weights. Workflows that need heavy weights will still cold-download on first call against a given volume; subsequent calls reuse via HF_HOME on the volume.",
    "--variant auto becoming the new default is non-breaking: scripts that pass --variant fresh keep working unchanged; scripts that omit --variant now get auto-selection.",
    "The runpod-lifecycle CLI is argparse-based with subparsers (validated by reading cli.py in Step 6.1). If it uses a different framework, Step 6.1 adapts accordingly without changing the plan's intent.",
    "extra_model_paths.yaml (or the ComfyUI fork's env-var equivalent) is the right way to redirect model lookups to a volume-hosted tree; if neither approach works for vibecomfy's ComfyUI fork, a symlink from the conventional path is the fallback (decided at implementation time after reading vibecomfy's loader code).",
    "test_primitives.py at the line numbers cited in the critique is current as of this revision; line numbers may shift with intervening commits but the literal assertion of the /opt path is the marker."
  ],

  {
    "id": "FLAG-001",
    "concern": "MooseFS venv performance: hosting the full cuda124 .venv on the existing MooseFS network volume (the only acceptable host per assumption #1) will likely add per-invocation Python-import overhead that the plan never benchmarks. The existing code intentionally puts the venv on container-local disk and sets UV_LINK_MODE=copy precisely because MooseFS is slow for many small files. Plan should either include a steady-state startup benchmark in Step 11, allocate the venv to a non-MooseFS persistent volume, or document the import-cost tradeoff.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "FLAG-002",
    "concern": "launch_command.build_run_worker_command hardcodes UV_PROJECT_ENVIRONMENT=/opt/reigh-worker-live-test-venv at line 37; the prebuilt variant must parameterize this or workers will launch against a different venv than bootstrap synced.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "FLAG-003",
    "concern": "Schema-version mismatch handling not differentiated from other drift. Plan treats schema_version drift via the same delta-sync code path that handles pyproject_hash drift, but schema_version bumps mean on-disk layout is incompatible and only a full rebuild via rl prebuilt build can fix it.",
    "status": "open",
    "severity": "minor"
  },
  {
    "id": "FLAG-004",
    "concern": "Concurrent builder collisions are unguarded. Two simultaneous rl prebuilt build invocations against the same named volume corrupt the cache with no lockfile/sentinel.",
    "status": "open",
    "severity": "minor"
  },
  {
    "id": "FLAG-005",
    "concern": "Builder pod orphan risk: prebuilt builder creates a pod but is not integrated with prune_stale_live_test_pods (which keys on the reigh-live-test-fresh- prefix). Builder crash between pod-create and terminate leaves an orphan that no existing reaper picks up.",
    "status": "open",
    "severity": "minor"
  },
  {
    "id": "FLAG-006",
    "concern": "Step 8.4 inverts the disk-size constraint. Moving content from ephemeral container disk to the network volume INCREASES persistent volume footprint, not decreases it. Plan should grow disk_size_gb / volume capacity, not shrink container disk in a way that conflates the two budgets.",
    "status": "open",
    "severity": "minor"
  },
  {
    "id": "FLAG-007",
    "concern": "Regional volume availability is unaddressed. RUNPOD_STORAGE_VOLUMES is a multi-region tuple precisely because RunPod volumes are region-pinned and GPU quota varies by region. Plan defaults to a single-name volume, which will fail pod-create whenever the GPU is allocated outside the volume region.",
    "status": "open",
    "severity": "minor"
  },
  {
    "id": "FLAG-008",
    "concern": "Model-cache reuse (the largest cost driver after deps) is not part of the prebuilt contract. The manifest tracks .venv and custom_nodes but does not declare or invalidate the HuggingFace / model weight cache that downloads on first workflow execution.     "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {

    "concern": "Did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: Idea requires the path to integrate cleanly with the existing live-test harness, RunPod lifecycle tools, workflow contracts, model reconciliation, and node-schema validation. Plan covers the bootstrap install seam and node-restore via lockfile hash but never explicitly addresses how model reconciliation (HuggingFace/model downloads) interacts with the prebuilt cache. Caching model weights on the volume is the largest single time/cost win for repeat runs and the plan is silent on whether the Peter volume already holds them, whether the manifest must track them, or what invalidates them.",

    "severity": "significant"

These are likely failure modes for a venv on a network filesystem and are not enumerated.",

    "severity": "significant"

    "concern": "Are the proposed changes technically correct?: Plan places the cached .venv at /workspace/prebuilt-livetest-venv on a MooseFS network volume. Hosting a full cuda124 venv (multi-GB of .so files imported at every worker startup) over MooseFS will incur a Python-import-time cost that the plan does not benchmark or even acknowledge.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"

  {
    "id": "correctness-2",
    "concern": "Are the proposed changes technically correct?: Plan says use contract.venv_path via UV_PROJECT_ENVIRONMENT so subsequent uv sync operations land in the cached venv (Step 7.4) but does not call out modifying build_run_worker_command in launch_command.py:37, which is the command actually used to spawn the worker process. Without parameterizing that builder, the worker would launch with the old /opt/ env var while bootstrap wrote to the prebuilt path, causing uv to re-resolve at runtime. Step 8.4 mentions only container/disk size overrides, not the launch command builder.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "correctness-3",
    "concern": "Are the proposed changes technically correct?: Plan delta-sync command for the worker is git fetch then git checkout ref then git reset --hard then uv sync, with no --extra cuda124. Current run_install deliberately passes --extra cuda124 at ssh_bootstrap.py:186. Without it the prebuilt path would silently drop the cuda extra, leading to wheel-shape drift or missing torch builds.     "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "correctness-4",
    "concern": "Are the proposed changes technically correct?: Plan stores python_version in the manifest (Step 1.1) but does NOT include it in the documented invalidation rule list (Step 1.2 only lists pyproject_hash, custom_nodes_lock_hash, comfyui_pin).   },
  {
    "id": "correctness-5",
    "concern": "Are the proposed changes technically correct?: Schema-version-bump equals mandatory rebuild (assumption #3) but the consumer flow in Step 7.2 only logs drift and either delta-syncs or aborts. Plan does not specify hard-fail-with-rebuild-command behavior for schema_version drift; the same code path treats it like any other field.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "correctness-6",
    "concern": "Are the proposed changes technically correct?: prune_stale_live_test_pods keys on the reigh-live-test-fresh- prefix at terminate_guard.py:14. The plan never specifies the pod-name prefix used by the new variant_prebuilt consumer pod nor by the builder pod from rl prebuilt build. If they reuse the fresh- prefix, prune will treat them as stale fresh pods; if they use a new prefix, the prune logic must be extended or stale prebuilt pods will accumulate.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "scope-1",
    "concern": "Search for related code that handles the same concept. Is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: Concurrent builders are unaddressed. rl prebuilt build writes the venv and manifest in-place on a named volume; two operators running it simultaneously against the same volume name would race on git reset --hard, pip install, and nodes restore. There is no lockfile, atomic rename, or in-progress sentinel in the manifest schema. Given the live-test workflow is multi-operator, this is a realistic failure mode the plan does not contain.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "scope-2",
    "concern": "Search for related code that handles the same concept. Is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: Builder ownership/termination is asymmetric. Step 5 says the builder provisions a pod with the named volume attached then terminates the pod (unless --keep-pod) with no integration into prune_stale_live_test_pods or guarded_terminate. If the builder crashes after pod-create but before terminate, the orphan pod is not reaped because the prune helper keys on the reigh-live-test-fresh- prefix. A standalone CLI verb still owns RunPod cost.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "scope-3",
    "concern": "Search for related code that handles the same concept. Is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: Plan adds a third entry point (variant_prebuilt.py) by cloning variant_fresh.py (Step 7.1). The success criterion reuses the matrix/queue/launch/report flow via shared helpers is a SHOULD, but the plan wording is clone variant_fresh as starting structure. variant_fresh.py is already ~500 lines; duplicating it instead of extracting shared _prepare_context / _validate_cases / _register_worker_record helpers creates a maintenance burden across three variants. The plan should commit to extraction explicitly, not as an aspiration.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "all_locations-1",
    "concern": "Does the change touch all locations AND supporting infrastructure?: Test fixtures in tests/test_primitives.py at lines 870, 2317-2319, 2445 assert the literal string UV_PROJECT_ENVIRONMENT=/opt/reigh-worker-live-test-venv. Step 3.4 promises no behavior change for variant_fresh.py - that survives - but variant_prebuilt will need an analogous test suite, and any future parameterization of launch_command.build_run_worker_command (needed per the correctness finding above) breaks all four assertions. The plan does not call out updating or branching these tests.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "all_locations-2",
    "concern": "Does the change touch all locations AND supporting infrastructure?: config.RUNPOD_STORAGE_VOLUMES is a tuple of region-specific candidates (Peter, EU-NO-1, EU-CZ-1, EUR-IS-1 per config.py:98). The plan default volume_name is reigh-livetest-prebuilt-portable, a single name with no region fallback. RunPod volumes are region-pinned and a 4090 quota allocation in a different region will fail pod create. The plan does not specify regional replicas, fallback-build behavior, or a region-pinned GPU constraint.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "all_locations-3",
    "concern": "Does the change touch all locations AND supporting infrastructure?: Adding the new prebuilt CLI verb (Step 5) requires wiring into runpod-lifecycle/src/runpod_lifecycle/cli.py subparser registry. The plan does not enumerate that touchpoint nor say where the verb is registered (build_parser style or subcommand dispatch).",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "all_locations-4",
    "concern": "Does the change touch all locations AND supporting infrastructure?: Skill discovery: Step 12.4 mentions adding a reference auto-memory entry but auto-memory is per-user, not committed to the repo; it cannot satisfy future agents discover and use this path by default for agents running on other hosts. The doc/skill addition is the only durable surface and the plan is vague on what closest existing skill means.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "callers-1",
    "concern": "Find the callers of the changed function. What arguments do they actually pass? Does the fix handle all of them?: Callers of run_install: variant_fresh.py:439 only. Callers of clone_and_install_vibecomfy: variant_fresh.py:448 and variant_update.py:35 import. The plan Step 3 refactor preserves public signatures, so variant_fresh is safe. But variant_update.py has its own REMOTE_UV_SYNC block (variant_update.py:69-79) that uses --locked and writes to /opt/ - the plan never says whether update-mode should also adopt prebuilt or how the two paths coexist if an operator switches between them.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "callers-2",
    "concern": "Find the callers of the changed function. What arguments do they actually pass? Does the fix handle all of them?: vibecomfy.cli nodes restore --lockfile custom_nodes.lock is invoked unconditionally in current clone_and_install_vibecomfy at ssh_bootstrap.py:231. Plan ensure_vibecomfy_synced delta path is described as mirroring the same delta logic but the node-restore step is destructive (re-clones custom_nodes). Plan does not specify whether delta mode skips restore (risk: lockfile drift unfixed) or always runs it (risk: re-clones add minutes when nothing changed). The manifest stores custom_nodes_lock_hash but does not say it gates the restore call.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "callers-3",
    "concern": "Find the callers of the changed function. What arguments do they actually pass? Does the fix handle all of them?: Plan Step 8.4 says reduce the live-test container/disk size for the prebuilt variant since .venv lives on the volume - this inverts the constraint. Moving content from ephemeral container disk to the network volume INCREASES persistent volume footprint. The project memory note project_live_test_disk.md already records that 50GB is too small for WAN 14B; the prebuilt path needs MORE volume headroom, not less container disk.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  }
]

  "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-007",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",

      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-106",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-107",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-143",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the path-mapping defect from the previous iteration is fixed, but a different technical gap remains around `self_refiner`. repository search shows `shared.utils.self_refiner` is imported by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/distilled.py`, `ti2vid_one_stage.py`, `ti2vid_two_stages.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py`, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`; the v4 plan still aligns `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` to upstream head without any concrete runtime step that exercises those code paths after the change, so the only repo-documented behavior check for that file is still missing from the executable body.",

      "id": "DEBT-155",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: a residual issue-hint tension remains for resolution handling. the plan acknowledges current `vibecomfy run` lacks a resolution flag, but allows the adapter to leave resolution and other template-specific values unwired for sprint 2. because `z_image_turbo` and `qwen_image_2512` are marked vibecomfy-supported direct routes, this may not preserve the existing worker task-parameter contract for tasks that provide a non-default `resolution`.",

  "does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-014",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: phase 1 still misses a real supporting-data location: `trailingpairdata` lives outside `pairdatabyindex` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts#l111), but the proposed sync logic only considers `pairdatabyindex`. if trailing slot regenerate is part of the bug surface, that location is not covered.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-110",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the non-swallowing helper `update_worker_phase_strict` is a new shell function, but phase 1 step 2 introduces it only as pseudo-code. there is no concrete note that the helper must handle supabase http non-2xx responses as failures (curl returns 0 even on http 4xx/5xx by default unless `-f` or `--fail-with-body` is used). if the implementer ports `update_worker_phase` verbatim with only `|| return 0` removed, a 500 from supabase would still report success because curl's exit code is 0. the plan should specify `curl --fail` or explicit http status code checking in the strict variant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-142",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the supporting-infrastructure review still finds one missing check. in `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md`, sprint 2's verification matrix and risk register both single out `self_refiner` as a distinct drift-upgrade surface with its own smoke and its own silent-behavior-change risk, but the revised v4 body updates the file without keeping that supporting verification step in the actual execution checklist.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-154",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: documentation/data gap: the sprint exit criteria require profiles 1 and 3 to have vram and wall-clock data, but the plan puts that only in metadata as an info-level criterion and does not schedule a concrete artifact location or command for recording the measurements. the implementation could satisfy all unit tests while still missing one of the explicit exit criteria.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-1-vibecomfy-memory-20260506-0147"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-158",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the supporting infrastructure for resolution is still incomplete if direct vibecomfy routes are considered production-supported. vibecomfy has a `vibecomfy.patches.resolution` helper and the cli can load scratchpad files with `allow_scratchpad=true`, but the plan does not require using that path; it permits leaving resolution out for sprint 2.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-2-adapter-seam-and-20260506-0249"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-162",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: checked vibecomfy profile wording against `vibecomfy/tests/test_ready_templates.py`. the revised plan says to add the production template to representative compile profile coverage 'alongside the existing dry-run id', but the dry-run id is not currently in `profile_smoke_template_ids`; only `video/wanvideo_wrapper_22_5b_i2v` and `video/wan_t2v` are. this is a small wording mismatch rather than a functional blocker because adding either the production id alone or both ids would still satisfy sprint 4.",

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-109",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-146",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: finding callers of the changed module shows that `shared.utils.self_refiner` is consumed by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx2_handler.py`, multiple ltx2 pipeline modules, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`. the revised validation steps still only drive the three bridge getters in `source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py`; none of the scheduled commands execute a real `self_refiner` caller, so the plan does not yet verify the main caller shapes for the other explicit sprint 2 file change.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-159",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked direct queue caller values in `db_task_to_generation_task()`: direct image tasks may carry `resolution`, `steps`/`num_inference_steps`, `seed`, and `override_profile`. the revised adapter plan covers seed, steps, and memory profile but not guaranteed resolution propagation, so it does not handle all relevant direct-route caller arguments for a supported vibecomfy direct route.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-2-adapter-seam-and-20260506-0249"
      ]
    }
  ],

        Escalated debt subsystems:
        [
  {
    "subsystem": "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct",
    "total_occurrences": 9,
    "plan_count": 6,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-007",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-008",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-018",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-106",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-107",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-143",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the path-mapping defect from the previous iteration is fixed, but a different technical gap remains around `self_refiner`. repository search shows `shared.utils.self_refiner` is imported by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/distilled.py`, `ti2vid_one_stage.py`, `ti2vid_two_stages.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py`, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`; the v4 plan still aligns `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` to upstream head without any concrete runtime step that exercises those code paths after the change, so the only repo-documented behavior check for that file is still missing from the executable body.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-156",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the direct adapter still has a correctness risk around resolution parity. current vibecomfy ready templates hardcode image dimensions (`image/z_image` uses `emptysd3latentimage` 1024x1024 and `image/qwen_image_2512` uses 768x768), while `vibecomfy run` exposes no `--resolution` flag; the revised plan says a temporary scratchpad/input file may be generated only if supported, otherwise resolution is left out. that means a vibecomfy-supported direct route can silently ignore the worker's `resolution` param unless scratchpad resolution patching is made mandatory for supported direct routes or the support is explicitly limited to default-resolution smoke fixtures.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [

  {
    "subsystem": "does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure",
    "total_occurrences": 6,
    "plan_count": 6,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-014",
        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: phase 1 still misses a real supporting-data location: `trailingpairdata` lives outside `pairdatabyindex` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts#l111), but the proposed sync logic only considers `pairdatabyindex`. if trailing slot regenerate is part of the bug surface, that location is not covered.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-110",
        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the non-swallowing helper `update_worker_phase_strict` is a new shell function, but phase 1 step 2 introduces it only as pseudo-code. there is no concrete note that the helper must handle supabase http non-2xx responses as failures (curl returns 0 even on http 4xx/5xx by default unless `-f` or `--fail-with-body` is used). if the implementer ports `update_worker_phase` verbatim with only `|| return 0` removed, a 500 from supabase would still report success because curl's exit code is 0. the plan should specify `curl --fail` or explicit http status code checking in the strict variant.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-142",
        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the supporting-infrastructure review still finds one missing check. in `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md`, sprint 2's verification matrix and risk register both single out `self_refiner` as a distinct drift-upgrade surface with its own smoke and its own silent-behavior-change risk, but the revised v4 body updates the file without keeping that supporting verification step in the actual execution checklist.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-154",
        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: documentation/data gap: the sprint exit criteria require profiles 1 and 3 to have vram and wall-clock data, but the plan puts that only in metadata as an info-level criterion and does not schedule a concrete artifact location or command for recording the measurements. the implementation could satisfy all unit tests while still missing one of the explicit exit criteria.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "sprint-1-vibecomfy-memory-20260506-0147"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-158",
        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the supporting infrastructure for resolution is still incomplete if direct vibecomfy routes are considered production-supported. vibecomfy has a `vibecomfy.patches.resolution` helper and the cli can load scratchpad files with `allow_scratchpad=true`, but the plan does not require using that path; it permits leaving resolution out for sprint 2.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "sprint-2-adapter-seam-and-20260506-0249"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-162",
        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: checked vibecomfy profile wording against `vibecomfy/tests/test_ready_templates.py`. the revised plan says to add the production template to representative compile profile coverage 'alongside the existing dry-run id', but the dry-run id is not currently in `profile_smoke_template_ids`; only `video/wanvideo_wrapper_22_5b_i2v` and `video/wan_t2v` are. this is a small wording mismatch rather than a functional blocker because adding either the production id alone or both ids would still satisfy sprint 4.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "sprint-4-wan-single-frame-and-20260506-0818"
        ]

  {
    "subsystem": "did-the-work-fully-address-the-issue-hints-user-notes-and-approved-plan-requirements",
    "total_occurrences": 5,
    "plan_count": 5,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-013",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the package is internally inconsistent. the plan body says to keep `activepairdata` in `usestate` and make phase 2 optional, but the attached metadata and success criteria still describe the previous derive-via-`usememo` / remove-`setactivepairdata` / simplify-`onpairclick` plan. that means the approved-plan requirements are only partially coherent as presented.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-108",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the linux distro note is documentation-only; the generated install command in step 7 still runs `apt-get install python3.10-venv python3.10-dev ffmpeg` without any pre-check that the package exists. users on ubuntu 24.04+ who ignore the doc and try to copy-paste the command will hit a confusing `e: unable to locate package python3.10-venv` from apt rather than a targeted error from commandutils.ts. a one-line detection pre-check (e.g., `apt-cache show python3.10-venv >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo 'install deadsnakes ppa first \u2014 see readme'; exit 1; }`) would convert the silent failure into an actionable error, but the plan does not add this.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-145",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: checked the revised v4 body against `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md` sprint 2. the repo plan doc still lists an \"ltx-2 `self_refiner` smoke against the pre-sprint behavioral baseline\" as a sprint 2 verification gate and repeats that smoke in the functionality-preservation checks, but the revised execution steps no longer schedule that smoke anywhere; it survives only as an info-level metadata criterion. because `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` remains an explicit sprint 2 deliverable, the revised plan still only partially carries forward the verification package described in the source migration plan.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-153",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: worker integration: the migration doc describes the per-call profile override gap as involving reigh-worker adapter handling of override_profile before constructing vibecomfy sessionconfig, but the plan limits sprint 1 per-run work to the vibecomfy cli/runtime. that may be acceptable for a vibecomfy-library mvp, but it does not fully exercise the production-facing per-task override semantics called out in the overall context.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "sprint-1-vibecomfy-memory-20260506-0147"

        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: a residual issue-hint tension remains for resolution handling. the plan acknowledges current `vibecomfy run` lacks a resolution flag, but allows the adapter to leave resolution and other template-specific values unwired for sprint 2. because `z_image_turbo` and `qwen_image_2512` are marked vibecomfy-supported direct routes, this may not preserve the existing worker task-parameter contract for tasks that provide a non-default `resolution`.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,

> AGENT

Critique iteration 2 complete. Most prior flags genuinely addressed — verified FLAG-001 through FLAG-007 (bundle-extract architecture moves venv to container disk, launch_command parameterized, --extra cuda124 mandatory and tested, python_version/schema_version are hard-fail invalidation triggers, builder pod prefix and prune integration, build.lock with TTL, and disk-size constraint corrected). FLAG-008 (model cache) is structurally addressed but warming is deferred — left unverified.

New significant concerns introduced by the revision:

- **FLAG-009 — `/workspace-local` path is invented.** Plan sets `runtime_worker_path = /workspace-local/reigh-worker` as "container disk" but no such mountpoint exists in the runpod base image. Intent is almost certainly `/opt/` (matching the existing `/opt/reigh-worker-live-test-venv` pattern).
- **FLAG-010 — `--python 3.10` still hardcoded in launch_command.py:43-44.** Manifest's python_version is meant to be a hard-fail invalidation trigger, but the worker spawn never reads it; a py3.11 prebuilt venv launched with `--python 3.10` defeats the invariant.
- **FLAG-014 — HF_HOME not threaded into worker env.** `bind_models_dir` sets it in the SSH session only; the launched worker subprocess uses `export_env(worker_env)` from `_build_worker_env`, which the plan doesn't update — so models would still re-download.
- **FLAG-011 — `comfyui.tar.zst` is redundant.** ComfyUI is pip-installed into the venv site-packages (no separate install tree exists).
- **FLAG-012 — Region-suffix derivation is wrong.** `RUNPOD_STORAGE_VOLUMES = ('Peter', 'EU-NO-1', ...)` — 'Peter' is a volume name, not a region. Should derive from `dataCenterId`.
- **FLAG-013 — `find_gpu_type` has no region filter** (api.py:83-97); the optimistic "if possible" in Step 8.2 should be dropped.
- **FLAG-015 — verify_extracted_env runs before sync.** A network drop mid-`uv sync` produces partial-state the probe already cleared.
- **FLAG-016 — Silent default change** of `--variant` to `auto` deserves documentation/changelog.
- **FLAG-017 — Wrong import direction.** runpod-lifecycle is the standalone package; reigh-worker depends on it, so the "import terminate_guard from reigh-worker" branch in Step 6.5 is structurally wrong.

Plus three scope/all-locations findings (shared helper rename, variant_update coexistence on shared pod, `_FRESH_POD_NAME_RE` regex needs broadening), and three caller findings (dry-run path, sync ordering, register_worker_record args contract).

# Implementation Plan: Prebuilt RunPod/VibeComfy Validation Environment (rev 3 — fixups)

## Overview

This revision is a **surgical fixup pass against rev 2**, not another architectural pivot. The bundle-extract architecture (settled decision ARCH-001) is preserved: compressed `tar.zst` bundles live on the RunPod network volume; the consumer pod extracts the venv + vibecomfy install tree to **container-local disk** at boot. The 24 open significant flags from the rev 2 critique are all concrete file/line/decision issues that we correct in place.

**Key corrections folded into this revision:**

1. **Container-disk paths corrected** (correctness-1 / FLAG-009): replace invented `/workspace-local/*` with `/opt/reigh-livetest-prebuilt/{worker,vibecomfy}` — consistent with the existing `/opt/reigh-worker-live-test-venv` convention. `/workspace` remains the network-volume mountpoint.
2. **ComfyUI bundle merged into venv bundle** (correctness-2): ComfyUI is pip-installed into the venv (`ssh_bootstrap.py:225-227`), so it lives inside `site-packages`. Only **two** bundles: `venv.cuda124.tar.zst` and `vibecomfy.tar.zst`. The `extract_comfyui_bundle` phase is removed.
3. **`--python` in launch_command parameterized** (correctness-7 / FLAG-010 / all_locations-1): `build_run_worker_command` now takes `python_version` (default `3.10` for the fresh path, manifest-driven for prebuilt). `launch_command.py:43-44` is an enumerated touch point.
4. **HF cache env threaded into `_build_worker_env`** (FLAG-014 / scope-3): `HF_HOME`, `HF_HUB_CACHE`, and the ComfyUI models-path env are added to the worker-env dict that `export_env` serializes, so the worker subprocess inherits them — not just the SSH bootstrap shell.
5. **Region detection via `get_network_volumes()` `dataCenterId`** (correctness-3): `PREBUILT_VOLUME_CANDIDATES` is no longer derived from the legacy `RUNPOD_STORAGE_VOLUMES` name tuple. Instead the consumer enumerates `get_network_volumes(api_key)`, filters by the `reigh-livetest-prebuilt-{profile}-` prefix, and selects by `dataCenterId`.
6. **`find_gpu_type` region wording dropped** (correctness-4): the SDK has no region filter (`runpod-lifecycle/src/runpod_lifecycle/api.py:83-97`). The plan now states the actual mechanism — RunPod pod creation enforces region match implicitly through the attached volume's `dataCenterId`; we let pod-create fail loudly rather than pretending to constrain.
7. **`verify_extracted_env` reordered after syncs and made crash-resistant** (callers-2 / correctness-5): the probe phase runs **after** `sync_worker_ref` and `sync_vibecomfy_ref`, catches non-zero exit codes from each shell probe and translates them to diagnostic strings rather than letting them raise.
8. **Cross-repo import direction fixed** (correctness-6): the prune helper lives in `runpod-lifecycle/src/runpod_lifecycle/guard.py` as `prune_pods_by_prefix(prefixes, api_key, ...)`. `reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/terminate_guard.py` becomes a thin caller. There is no fallback branch — runpod-lifecycle never imports reigh-worker code.
9. **`_register_fresh_worker_record` rename committed** (scope-1 / callers-3): renamed to `register_worker_record(..., variant_label: str)` in `_shared.py`. `variant_fresh.py` passes `variant_label="fresh"`; `variant_prebuilt.py` passes `"prebuilt"`. Both call sites enumerated.
10. **variant_update coexistence guarded** (scope-2): if `variant_update.run` finds a prebuilt manifest at `/workspace/reigh-livetest-prebuilt/env.manifest.json` it refuses with an actionable error (`use --variant prebuilt or invalidate the prebuilt cache first`). This is a one-line check; no other changes to variant_update.
11. **`_FRESH_POD_NAME_RE` enumerated for all three prefixes** (all_locations-2): the regex becomes a tuple of three compiled patterns, each scoped to one prefix, all using the same timestamp suffix `%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ` (the existing format from `_timestamp_label()`).
12. **`test_primitives.py` import-path updates enumerated** (all_locations-3): test imports of `_phase` / `_redact_sensitive_text` / `_capture_and_redact_noisy_lifecycle_output` get re-exports from `variant_fresh` so existing tests still resolve them. (`from scripts.live_test._shared import _phase as _phase` line added to `variant_fresh.py` keeps the old import path working.)
13. **`__all__` updated** (all_locations-4): `config.py:__all__` is extended to export `PREBUILT_VOLUME_CANDIDATES`, `PREBUILT_CACHE_ROOT`, `PREBUILT_RUNTIME_VENV_PATH`, `PREBUILT_RUNTIME_WORKER_PATH`, `PREBUILT_RUNTIME_VIBECOMFY_PATH`.
14. **Default `--variant` stays `fresh`** (issue_hints-2): the rev 2 plan flipped the default to `auto`; reverted here. `--variant auto` is opt-in. Future flip can be a separate migration with release-note coverage. Skill + docs still recommend `--variant auto`, which is the durable discovery story.
15. **Node-schema validation runs on every prebuilt run** (issue_hints-3): a cheap read-only `python -m vibecomfy.cli nodes verify --lockfile custom_nodes.lock` (or equivalent template_index sanity probe) runs in the bootstrap regardless of lockfile drift. Destructive `nodes restore` still gated on `custom_nodes_lock_hash` drift.
16. **Dry-run plan honors variant venv path** (callers-1): `_print_dry_run_plan` (or `variant_prebuilt._print_dry_run_plan`) takes `venv_path` and `python_version` from the contract so dry-run output for the prebuilt variant displays the correct paths.
17. **Model warming product call** (issue_hints-1 / FLAG-008): accept v1 first-run model cold-download as in-scope cost; the prebuilt env materially improves *deps* time (67 min → ~10 min) and **steady-state** model time (subsequent runs reuse `models/` via `HF_HOME`). A future `rl prebuilt warm-models <recipe>` verb is explicitly deferred to v2 and tracked as an open question, not a v1 requirement.

**Updated key-file touchpoints (additions over rev 2 enumerated):**

- `reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/launch_command.py:37, 43-44` (add `venv_path` and `python_version` kwargs)
- `runpod-lifecycle/src/runpod_lifecycle/guard.py` (new — houses `prune_pods_by_prefix`)
- `reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/variant_update.py` (add prebuilt-manifest-present guard only)
- `reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/_shared.py` (now also owns `register_worker_record`, re-exports preserved for test compat)
- `reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/config.py:__all__` (export new prebuilt constants)
- `reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/variant_fresh.py` (compatibility re-exports for `_phase`, `_redact_sensitive_text`, `_capture_and_redact_noisy_lifecycle_output`)

**Decisions explicitly settled (not re-litigated here):** ARCH-001 through ARCH-007 from the gate's settled-decisions list — bundle architecture, hard-fail invalidation triggers, pod-name prefixes, variant_update out of scope, build.lock concurrency, helper extraction before new variant, committed skill manifest as discovery surface.
